- **eo-doc-manager**: 管理 eo-doc/ 代码侧文档体系（init / modify / sync）：维护 changes/INDEX.md、agent-handbook/ 规范篇与 templates/；`state.enabled` 时cursor 增量再生 state/ 现状篇。所有 eo-doc 下的文档维护操作必须走此 skill。触发：初始化文档 / 修改文档 / 整理文档 / 同步文档 / /eo-doc-manager。NOT FOR：查询与解释文档内容（走 /eo-recall）。 ([SimpleEve/eo-skills](https://github.com/SimpleEve/eo-skills/tree/HEAD/eo-doc-manager) · ★31)

- **eo-implement**: 按 change.md 的 TODO 分批落地代码，批末自验对应 AC。触发：实现 / 写代码 / implement / /eo-implement。 NOT FOR: bug 与反馈修复（口喷 bug 与 test/review/acceptance 循环内反馈一律走 /eo-fix）；变更起草（走 /eo-change）。 ([SimpleEve/eo-skills](https://github.com/SimpleEve/eo-skills/tree/HEAD/eo-implement) · ★31)

- **epe-1-explore**: EXPLORE phase of the EPE cycle. Takes the story (or brief) of the active change, validates it is complete, checks EACH acceptance criterion against what already exists (repo code, dependencies, primary sources) citing file:line or URL with date, detects non-functional constraints and classifies the change. Writes exploration.md. Does not plan or edit. Use when the user says "/epe-1-explore", "explore this story", "does this already exist?", "research before writing", or brings acceptance criteria. ([earhackerdem/GlobalHealth](https://github.com/earhackerdem/GlobalHealth/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/epe-1-explore))
- **epe-2-plan**: PLAN phase of the EPE cycle. From an accepted exploration.md produces plan.md (goal, scope and non-scope, design, files with anchor, tickets, ONE test per criterion, constraints addressed, risks, phases) and tasks.md. Gate: nothing is edited until the user writes "plan approved"; then it marks `Status: APPROVED` and the gate-plan hook allows editing. Use when the user says "/epe-2-plan", "make the plan", "plan approved", or after accepting an exploration. ([earhackerdem/GlobalHealth](https://github.com/earhackerdem/GlobalHealth/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/epe-2-plan))
- **epe-3-execute**: EXECUTE phase of the EPE cycle. With an APPROVED plan.md, implements (or produces) tasks.md in vertical increments on its own branch, verifies each criterion with its test until truly green, writes evidence.md and validation.md (non-technical, for whoever approves), leaves a journal entry and opens the PR with the template. Never merges, never deploys, never pushes to protected branches. Use when the user says "/epe-3-execute", "implement the plan", "execute", "write the piece", after "plan approved". ([earhackerdem/GlobalHealth](https://github.com/earhackerdem/GlobalHealth/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/epe-3-execute))
